4:Tc8a,(() => {
  const value = {"keys":{"preset":"theme_preset","font":"theme_font","radius":"theme_radius","scale":"theme_scale","contentLayout":"theme_content_layout"},"allowed":{"preset":["default","anthropic","simple-large","underground","rose-garden","lake-view","sunset-glow","forest-whisper","ocean-breeze","lavender-dream"],"font":["default","sans","serif"],"radius":["default","none","sm","md","lg","xl"],"scale":["default","sm","lg","xl"],"contentLayout":["full","centered"]},"defaults":{"preset":"default","font":"default","radius":"default","scale":"default","contentLayout":"full"},"fonts":{"default":"sans","anthropic":"serif","simple-large":"sans","underground":"sans","rose-garden":"sans","lake-view":"sans","sunset-glow":"sans","forest-whisper":"sans","ocean-breeze":"sans","lavender-dream":"sans"},"modeKey":"vite-ui-theme"};
  const cookies = new Map(document.cookie.split(';').map((entry) => {
    const index = entry.indexOf('=');
    return [entry.slice(0, index).trim(), entry.slice(index + 1)];
  }));
  const root = document.documentElement;
  let mode = cookies.get(value.modeKey);
  if (mode === undefined) {
    try { mode = localStorage.getItem(value.modeKey) ?? 'system'; }
    catch { mode = 'system'; }
  }
  if (mode !== 'light' && mode !== 'dark' && mode !== 'system') mode = 'system';
  // Mirror the validated cookie-first choice for next-themes' React state.
  // Its separate inline script is inert; restricted storage must not block paint.
  try {
    if (localStorage.getItem(value.modeKey) !== mode) localStorage.setItem(value.modeKey, mode);
  } catch { /* The cookie-first bootstrap and hydration guard still apply. */ }
  const dark = mode === 'dark' ||
    (mode !== 'light' && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches);
  root.classList.remove('light', 'dark');
  root.classList.add(dark ? 'dark' : 'light');
  root.style.colorScheme = dark ? 'dark' : 'light';
  const attrs = {};
  // Keep stored customizations intact while this product uses one brand preset.
  const preset = value.defaults.preset;
  const fontValue = cookies.get(value.keys.font);
  const font = value.allowed.font.includes(fontValue) ? fontValue : value.defaults.font;
  attrs['data-theme-font'] = font === 'default' ? value.fonts[preset] : font;
  for (const key of ['radius', 'scale', 'contentLayout']) {
    const saved = cookies.get(value.keys[key]);
    const selected = value.allowed[key].includes(saved) ? saved : value.defaults[key];
    const attribute = 'data-theme-' + (key === 'contentLayout' ? 'content-layout' : key);
    if (key === 'contentLayout' || selected !== value.defaults[key]) attrs[attribute] = selected;
  }
  const apply = () => {
    if (!document.body) return;
    for (const key of ['preset', 'font', 'radius', 'scale', 'content-layout']) {
      const attribute = 'data-theme-' + key;
      if (attrs[attribute]) document.body.setAttribute(attribute, attrs[attribute]);
      else document.body.removeAttribute(attribute);
    }
  };
  if (document.body) apply();
  else {
    const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
      if (document.body) { apply(); observer.disconnect(); }
    });
    observer.observe(root, { childList: true });
  }
})();8:X
